Let's investigate exact square root flattening more. Given a target vector c_target (length 2n-1), recursive square root:
a[0]=sqrt(c_target[0])
for i=1..n-1:
  s = c_target[i] - sum_{j=1}^{i} 2*a[j]*a[i-j]
  a[i] = s/(2*a[0])
If a[0]>0. This yields coefficients of the unique square root with a0 positive if target coefficients up to n-1 are consistent. For i up to n-1, uses target first n. It ignores target beyond n?   compute c max, lse.

  it=0

    # g may be zero if beta small.
    gshift = g - np.min(g)
    # choose eta based on gshift max and a variation
    if gshift.max()>0:
       eta = 0.5 / (np.max(gshift) * max(beta? no g includes beta)? ) maybe.
    For line search:
       for factor in [0.25,0.5,1,2,4,8]? We want step.

Implementation of anti-diag direction for a set of lags:
def anti_direction(a, n, lags, alpha=0.5):
  d=np.zeros(n)
  for k in lags:
    k=int(k)
    # pairs i from 0 to k, valid j=k-i in [0,n-1]
    i0=max(0, k-(n-1)); i1=min(k, n-1)
    for i in range(i0,i1+1):
       j=k-i
       if i==j: continue? maybe handle by moving to global min? skip.
       if a[j] > a[i]:
          diff=a[j]-a[i]
          # transfer from i to j: d[i]-=alpha*diff; d[j]+=alpha*diff
       elif a[i] > a[j]:
          diff=a[i]-a[j]
          d[j]-=alpha*diff; d[i]+=alpha*diff
  return d
Loop over k and pairs total O(K*n). For K=50,n=8192 =>400k loops Python slow but okay per few iterations? We can vectorize for each k.

If diff>0, a[j]>a[i], transfer from i to j: d[i]-=amount, d[j]+=amount. If diff<0, a[i]>a[j], transfer from j to i: d[j]-=amount, d[i]+=amount. So d[i] += alpha*diff? compute_conv_full(a,N):
  n=len(a); N=int(N)
  if N <2*n-1: N=1 << (2*n-1).bit_length()
  a_pad=np.zeros(N); a_pad[:n]=a
  A=fft(a_pad)
  c=np.real(ifft(A*A))[:2*n-1]
  # numerical small imaginary discarded; maybe negative tiny from roundoff. Since true nonnegative, clip to 0? For cmax, negative tiny irrelevant. But for weights in LSE, exp beta*(c-cmax) with negative okay. Could clip c to 0 for safety: c=np.maximum(c,0).
  return c, A? For grads need A maybe. We can compute A inside lse_grad separately.

lse_grad from previous:
  def lse_grad(a,N,beta):
     n=len(a); Nf=int(N); if Nf<2n-1 adjust.
     a_pad; A=fft(a_pad); c=real(ifft(A*A))[:2n-1]; cmax=c.max();
     if beta<1e-6: weights = np.ones(len(c))/len(c); lse=cmax
     else: exp_bc = exp(beta*(c-cmax)); total=sum; weights=exp_bc/total; lse=cmax+log(total)/beta
     fsize = 1 << (3*n-2).bit_length()? Previous used fsize 1 << (3*n - 2).bit_length() for convolution of weights length 2n-1 and a length n? Need N for W*A product.      W = fft(weights, fsize)
     grad = 2*real(ifft(W * np.conj(Af)))[:n]

Mirror block implementation detail:
def mirror_lse_block(a_init,n,S,N,max_time,rng,beta_init=5,beta_end=100,momentum=0.0):
    a=safe_normalize(a_init,n,S)
    start=time.monotonic()
    best=a.copy(); best_cmax=inf
    # maybe maintain velocity in log? no.
    it=0; fail=0; eta=0.05
    beta=beta_init
    while time<max_time:
       it+=1
       if it%100==0: beta=min(beta_end,beta*1.2)
       g,lse,c,cmax=lse_grad(a,N,beta)
       if cmax<best_cmax: best=a.copy()
       if fail>40: a=spectral_partial_restart(a,N,rng,0.3); fail=0; continue? need recompute.
       gshift=g - np.min(g)
       gm=gshift.max()
       if gm <=1e-18: break
       # base eta maybe 0.2/gm? We want log ratio max ~eta*gm. Try base=0.3/gm so max ratio 0.3.
       base_eta=0.3/gm
       # line search over multipliers [0.1,0.25,0.5,1,2,4]
       accepted=False
       for mult in (0.1,0.25,0.5,1.0,2.0,4.0):
           eta_t=base_eta*mult
           logfac = -eta_t*gshift  # max 0
           # if min logfac < -700, underflow; clip
           logfac=np.maximum(logfac,-50)
           b=np.exp(logfac)
           b=b*a
           s=b.sum()
           if s<=1e-15: continue
           b=b*(S/s)
           c_b,_=compute_conv_full(b,N)
           cb=c_b.max()
           # also LSE maybe
           if cb <= cmax + 1e-12:
               a=b; c= c_b; cmax=cb; accepted=True
               if cb<best_cmax: best_cmax=cb; best=b.copy()
               # maybe update base_eta if mult>1?
               break
       if not accepted:
           # try smaller yet
           for mult in (0.05,0.02,0.01):
              ... same
           if accepted: fail=0 else fail+=1
       if it%500==0 and rng.random()<0.1: a=spectral_partial...; fail=0
    return best,best_cmax

Anti-diagonal block:
def anti_dir_block(a_init,n,S,N,max_time,rng,top_k=64,alpha=0.1):
  a=safe_normalize; best...
  fail=0
  it=0
  while time:
    c,_=compute_conv_full(a,N); cmax=c.max(); update best
    if fail>30: perturb and reset
    top=np.argsort(c)[::-1][:top_k]
    d=np.zeros(n)
    # vectorize over top lags
    for k in top:
       k=int(k)
       i0=max(0,k-(n-1)); i1=min(k,n-1)
       if i1<i0: continue
       i=np.arange(i0,i1+1)
       j=k-i
       # avoid i=j? include diff=0 no effect
       diff=a[j]-a[i]
       # amount = alpha * (magnitude) * sign(diff)
       # To avoid large moves, magnitude = 0.5*(a[i]+a[j]) maybe times alpha. For near zero diff random sign.
       mag=0.5*(a[i]+a[j])
       sign=np.sign(diff)
       # for sign==0, random sign; also for very small diff relative mag, random to break ties
       thr=1e-6*np.maximum(mag,1e-12)
       zero= (np.abs(diff) <= thr)
       if np.any(zero): sign[zero]=rng.choice([-1,1],size=zero.sum())
       amount=alpha*mag*sign
       np.add.at(d,i, -amount)
       np.add.at(d,j, amount)
    # sum d should ~0 but numerical; project_simplex will handle.
    # normalize direction: its l2 norm maybe large. step search
    dsum=d.sum(); d -= dsum/n? To preserve sum exactly. Since each pair cancels, sum zero; but multiple lags no issue. Subtract mean.
    norm=np.linalg.norm(d)
    if norm<1e-18: fail+=1; continue
    d = d/norm
    # base step: maybe 0.1*S/n? Direction after normalize has unit norm; step in l2. For changes maybe O(S/√n)=0.014? Actually S=128, norm unit, step=0.1 gives l2 0.1, small. But direction d may have entries large/norm ~? Could step 0.2*S? We can line search absolute step values [0.05*S,0.1*S,...]? If d normalized, step*l2 = step. Try steps = [0.1*S,0.05*S,0.02*S,0.01*S,0.2*S,0.5*S]? Use project_simplex.
    accepted=False
    for step in [0.05*S,0.1*S,0.02*S,0.01*S,0.2*S,1.0*S]:
       cand=project_simplex(a+step*d,S)
       cc,_=compute_conv_full(cand,N); cb=cc.max()
       if cb <= cmax+1e-12:
          a=cand; c=cc; cmax=cb; accepted=True; update best; break
    if not accepted:
       for step in [0.005*S,0.002*S]:...
       if not: fail++
    if fail>20: a = a*perturb; normalize
  return best

This direction may be too random; top K=64, vectorized loops 64*8192=524k, okay. Each iteration does FFT. Could do maybe 100 iterations in 50s.

LP block:
def lp_block(a_init,n,S,N,max_time,rng,maxK=80):
  a=safe_norm; best...
  it=0
  while time:
    c,cmax; update
    K=current min(maxK, 40+it*2)
    top=np.argsort(c)[::-1][:K]
    # build G matrix K x n: row r for k: 2*a[(k-i) valid]
    A_equiv = np.zeros((K,n+1))? Actually linprog constraints -G δ - t <= -c.
    cvals=c[top]
    M=np.zeros((K,n))
    for idx,k in enumerate(top):
       i0=max(0,k-(n-1)); i1=min(k,n-1)
       # G_i = 2*a[k-i] for i in valid
       M[idx,i0:i1+1] = 2*a[k - np.arange(i0,i1+1)]
    A_ub = np.empty((K+1,n+1))
    A_ub[:K,:n] = -M
    A_ub[:K,n] = -1.0
    b_ub = -cvals.copy() for first K; for equality? linprog separate A_eq.
    A_eq = np.zeros((1,n+1)); A_eq[0,:n]=1; b_eq=0
    bounds=[( -a_i, max_inc) for i] + [(0, None)]
    max_inc = 0.1*S? But if a_i small, upper small? Use max_inc=0.2*S maybe.
    try res=linprog(c_obj, A_ub, b_ub, A_eq, b_eq, bounds, method='highs')
    if res.success:
       delta=res.x[:n]
       # line search along delta
       for step in [1,0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05,0.02]:
          cand=project_simplex(a+step*delta,S)
          evaluate; if improve accept
       # if no improve, maybe use -delta? No.
    else fail
    if fail>20: perturb
  return best

Maybe we can exploit gradient formula to build anti direction? For peak k, gradient is 2 shifted a. Steepest descent direction (sum fixed) is shift a - mean. Anti-direction differs by sign? Let's compare: For k, gradient high at i if a_{k-i} high. Moving mass from high gradient to low gradient reduces c_k. This means moving from i where partner high to i where partner low. Equivalent to for each pair (i,j) summing k, if a_j > a_i (partner j high for i, partner i low for j), gradient at i=2a_j high, gradient at j=2a_i low; move from i to j (smaller to larger). Exactly anti-direction! Great. So anti-direction is steepest descent for a single active peak with a special symmetric move (since each pair gets move in opposite directions). It is actually the projected negative gradient for the single lag if we restrict moves to pairs and equal amount? The full gradient vector G_i=2a_{k-i}; direction -G + mean. For pair i,j, G_i=2a_j, G_j=2a_i. Negative gradient would move from high G (i with larger a_j -> smaller a_i) to low G (j with larger a_i -> larger a_j): from smaller to larger. The amount for gradient descent could be proportional to difference of G: 2(a_j-a_i). Our anti amount α*mag*sign, not exactly proportional; we can use amount = α*diff (or α*(a_j-a_i)) which is exactly half gradient difference and makes more unequal. For diff positive, d_i -= α*diff, d_j += α*diff. This is the symmetric part of -G. If we use α=1, new pair sum fixed, products? For diff positive: a_i' = (1-α)a_i+α a_j? Wait d_i=-α diff, d_j=α diff. If diff=a_j-a_i, a_i' = a_i -α(a_j-a_i) = (1+α)a_i - α a_j. For α>0, if a_j>a_i, a_i decreases (could negative), a_j increases. Difference becomes (1+2α)diff. Product decreases. Good. The gradient descent vector -G sum zero? ∑G=2∑a=2S, mean =2S/n; direction -G+2S/n. For pair, this includes -2a_j+const and -2a_i+const; not exactly pair transfer, but within pair relative difference is -2a_j -(-2a_i)=-2(a_j-a_i). Our transfer amount proportional diff. Good.

def active_peak_block(a_init,n,S,N,max_time,rng,top_k=32):
  for it:
    c,cmax
    top=sort c desc [:top_k]
    weights: maybe uniform or exp(β(c-cmax)) among top (β small 0.5?); G=np.zeros(n)
    for k in top:
      G += w_k * 2*shifted_a(k)
    G -= G.mean()  # ensure zero sum
    # direction to reduce active = -G? If G gradient of weighted sum, to minimize use -G.
    d = -G

Implementation of shifted gradient for k:
def shifted_grad(a,k):
  n=len(a); out=np.zeros(n)
  i0=max(0,k-(n-1)); i1=min(k,n-1)
  out[i0:i1+1] = 2*a[k - np.arange(i0,i1+1)]
  return out
This is derivative of c_k (without weights). For multiple, sum.

Let's implement peak_steepest_block:
def peak_steepest_block(a_init,n,S,N,max_time,rng,top_k_start=4,top_k_end=64):
    a=safe_normalize(a_init,n,S)
    start=time.monotonic()
    best=a.copy(); best_cmax=inf
    momentum=np.zeros(n); it=0; fail=0
    while time.monotonic()-start < max_time:
       it+=1
       c,_=compute_conv_full(a,N); cmax=c.max()
       if cmax<best_cmax: best_cmax=cmax; best=a.copy()
       K=min(top_k_end, top_k_start + it//20)
       top=np.argsort(c)[::-1][:K]
       # weights: uniform or exponential with beta=0.2? Use uniform for active.
       # compute G
       G=np.zeros(n)
       # for each k in top, add 2*shifted
       for k in top:
          k=int(k)
          i0=max(0,k-(n-1)); i1=min(k,n-1)
          # G[i0:i1+1] += 2*a[k - np.arange(i0,i1+1)]
          G[i0:i1+1] += 2.0 * a[k - np.arange(i0,i1+1)]
       G -= G.mean()
       # normalize direction? project_search uses step relative.
       norm=np.linalg.norm(G)
       if norm<1e-18: fail+=1; continue
       d = -G / norm
       # momentum optional: d = 0.8*d +0.2*mom; mom=d
       # line search steps: base scale = S maybe; try [0.01,0.03,0.1,0.3,1.0,3.0] * S? Since d unit norm, step S gives l2 128, huge; projection may ruin. Try [0.002,0.005,0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1,0.3] * S.
       accepted=False
       for scale in (0.002,0.005,0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1,0.3):
          step=scale*S
          cand=project_simplex(a+step*d,S)
          cc,_=compute_conv_full(cand,N); cb=cc.max()
          if cb <= cmax + 1e-12:
             a=cand; c=cc; cmax=cb; accepted=True
             if cb<best_cmax: best_cmax=cb; best=cand.copy()
             break
       if not accepted:
          for scale in (0.0005,0.001):...
          if not: fail+=1
       if fail>30:
          # perturb
          noise=rng.normal(0,0.02,a.size)
          a=a*np.exp(noise); a=safe_normalize(a,n,S)
          fail=0
    return best,best_cmax
```
This is projected gradient with normalized G. It may be slow line search 7 evals/it. Could reduce line search: first try step=0.01*S, if not improve try 0.003,0.03. Good.

 def beta_family_sequence(n,p,q):
   i=np.arange(n,dtype=float)
   loga=(p-1)*np.log(i+0.5)+(q-1)*np.log(n-i-0.5)
   loga-=loga.max()
   a=np.exp(loga)
   return a
This avoids overflow. For p-1 negative and i+0.5 positive no issue. Normalize later.

Let's carefully implement project_simplex:
def project_simplex(v, S):
    n=len(v)
    if n==0: return v
    v=np.asarray(v,dtype=np.float64)
    if S<=0: return np.zeros(n)
    w=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cumsum=np.cumsum(w)
    idx=np.arange(1,n+1,dtype=np.float64)
    theta=(cumsum-S)/idx
    valid=theta <= w
    if not np.any(valid):
       return np.maximum(v,0)* (S/np.maximum(np.sum(np.maximum(v,0)),1e-300))? But if no valid, rho=n? Standard:
       rho=n; theta=(cumsum[n-1]-S)/n
    else:
       rho=int(np.max(np.where(valid)[0])+1)
       theta=(cumsum[rho-1]-S)/rho
    return np.maximum(v-theta,0.0)
Actually if no valid, the algorithm with rho=n works? If theta_n > w_n, all? For projection onto sum, there is always valid if S positive? Could be if S > sum v? Then theta negative and valid true? If S huge, valid? Example v all -10, S=1, w=-10, cumsum negative, theta negative <= w? -? theta_1=-11 <= -10 true. So valid. No valid rare. We'll just if not any: return np.full(n,S/n).

safe_normalize:
def safe_normalize(a,n,S):
   a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
   if a.size != n:
      if a.size>n: a=a[:n]
      else: pad zeros
   a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
   s=a.sum()
   if s < 1e-12: return np.full(n,S/n)
   return a*(S/s)

compute_conv_full:
def compute_conv_full(a,N):
   n=len(a); N=int(N)
   if N < 2*n-1: N=1 << (2*n-1).bit_length()
   pad=np.zeros(N); pad[:n]=a
   A=fft(pad)
   c=np.real(ifft(A*A))[:2*n-1]
   c=np.maximum(c,0.0) # remove negatives
   return c,A? We only need c often. Could return c only. But lse_grad needs A? It computes separately. Return c to simplify.

But lse_grad uses compute? We'll implement standalone.

lse_grad:
def lse_grad(a,N,beta):
   n=len(a); Nf=int(N); if Nf<2*n-1 adjust.
   pad=zeros; A=fft(pad); c=real(ifft(A*A))[:2n-1]; c=np.maximum(c,0)
   cmax=float(c.max())
   if beta<=1e-6:
      weights=np.full(len(c),1.0/len(c)); lse=cmax
   else:
      x=beta*(c-cmax)
      # x <=0, no overflow; underflow to 0 okay
      ex=np.exp(x)
      tot=ex.sum()
      if tot<=0: weights=zeros; weights[argmax]=1
      else: weights=ex/tot
      lse=cmax+np.log(tot)/beta
   fsize=1 << (3*n-2).bit_length()
   W=fft(weights,fsize)
   Af=fft(a,fsize)
   grad=2.0*np.real(ifft(W*np.conj(Af)))[:n]
   return grad,lse,c,cmax

For beta large, weights may concentrate; fine.

```
def lbfgs_lse_block(a_init,n,S,N,max_time,rng,beta_init=5.0,beta_max=200.0,m_lbfgs=10,use_multi_beta=False):
    a=safe_normalize(a_init,n,S)
    beta=beta_init
    best=a.copy(); best_cmax=np.inf
    s_hist=[]; y_hist=[]
    if use_multi_beta:
        betas=[beta, beta*3, beta*9]
        g,c,c_max=multi_beta_grad(a,N,betas)
    else:
        g,_,c,c_max=lse_grad(a,N,beta)
    if c_max<best_cmax: ...
    step_scale=2.0*S/(n*max(beta,1.0))
    start=time.monotonic(); it=0
    while time.monotonic()-start < max_time:
        it+=1
        if it % 150 ==0:
            beta=min(beta_max,beta*1.1)
            step_scale=2.0*S/(n*max(beta,1.0))
            if use_multi_beta: ...
            else: ...
            if c_max<best_cmax: ...
        if it%40==0 and len(s_hist)>=m_lbfgs: s_hist=s_hist[-m_lbfgs:]; y_hist=...
        direction=lbfgs_dir(s_hist,y_hist,g)
        dir_norm=np.linalg.norm(direction)
        if dir_norm<1e-18: break
        alpha_ls=step_scale; a_new=a; cmax_new=c_max
        for ls in range(12):
            cand=project_simplex(a+alpha_ls*direction,S)
            c_ls=compute_conv_full(cand,N); cm=c_ls.max()
            if cm <= c_max or ls==11:
                a_new=cand; cmax_new=cm
                if cm <= c_max: break
            alpha_ls*=0.5
        if cmax_new<best_cmax: best...
        if use_multi_beta: g_new,c_new,cmax_nw=multi_beta_grad(...)
        else: g_new,_,c_new,cmax_nw=lse_grad(...)
        s_k=a_new-a; y_k=g_new-g; sy=dot(ss); if sy>...: append
        a=a_new; g=g_new; c_max=cmax_nw
        if it%600==0 and rng.random()<0.2:
            a=spectral_partial_restart(a,N,rng,0.3); s_hist=[]; y_hist=[]; recompute g...
    return best,best_cmax
```

```python
"""
Search for a non-negative step-function height sequence minimizing
    2 * n * max(np.convolve(a, a)) / sum(a)^2 .

Main ideas implemented here:
  * A cheap low-dimensional analytic seed sweep using Beta/kernel-profile
    families.  This produces strong non-random starting points.
  * Euclidean L-BFGS on a log-sum-exp smoothed max-convolution objective.
  * Projected exact-active-gradient (peak steepest) blocks.
  * Multiplicative/mirror LSE blocks for log-simplex refinement.
  * Nesterov top-k polishing.
  * Spectral-partial restarts and multi-start blending to escape local minima.

The final returned sequence is normalized internally, but the objective is
scale-invariant, so this is harmless.
"""

import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.fft import fft, ifft


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Basic helpers
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def project_simplex(v, S):
    """Euclidean projection onto {x >= 0, sum(x) = S}."""
    n = len(v)
    if n == 0:
        return v
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
    if S <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros(n)

    w = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cumsum = np.cumsum(w)
    idx = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    theta = (cumsum - S) / idx
    valid = theta <= w
    if not np.any(valid):
        rho = n
    else:
        rho = int(np.max(np.where(valid)[0]) + 1)
    theta = (cumsum[rho - 1] - S) / rho
    return np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)


def safe_normalize(a, n, S):
    """Clamp to valid non-negative range and rescale to sum S."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if a.size != n:
        tmp = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        m = min(n, a.size)
        tmp[:m] = a[:m]
        a = tmp
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-12:
        return np.full(n, S / n, dtype=np.float64)
    return a * (S / s)


def compute_conv_full(a, N):
    """Full linear self-convolution of a via FFT."""
    n = len(a)
    N = int(N)
    if N < 2 * n - 1:
        N = 1 << (2 * n - 1).bit_length()
    pad = np.zeros(N, dtype=np.float64)
    pad[:n] = a
    A = fft(pad)
    c = np.real(ifft(A * A))[:2 * n - 1]
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c


def lse_grad(a, N, beta):
    """
    Gradient of LSE(beta * c), where c = a * a, plus c and its maximum.

    Returns (grad, lse_value, c, cmax).
    """
    n = len(a)
    N = int(N)
    if N < 2 * n - 1:
        N = 1 << (2 * n - 1).bit_length()

    pad = np.zeros(N, dtype=np.float64)
    pad[:n] = a
    A = fft(pad)
    c = np.real(ifft(A * A))[:2 * n - 1]
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    cmax = float(c.max())

    if beta <= 1e-6:
        weights = np.full(len(c), 1.0 / len(c), dtype=np.float64)
        lse_val = cmax
    else:
        x = beta * (c - cmax)
        ex = np.exp(x)
        tot = float(ex.sum())
        if tot <= 1e-300:
            weights = np.zeros(len(c), dtype=np.float64)
            weights[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
            lse_val = cmax
        else:
            weights = ex / tot
            lse_val = cmax + np.log(tot) / beta

    fsize = 1 << (3 * n - 2).bit_length()
    W = fft(weights, fsize)
    Af = fft(a, fsize)
    grad = 2.0 * np.real(ifft(W * np.conj(Af)))[:n]
    grad = np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)
    return grad, lse_val, c, cmax


def multi_beta_grad(a, N, betas):
    """Weighted combination of LSE gradients at several beta values."""
    n = len(a)
    total = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    last_c = None
    denom = float(len(betas) * (len(betas) + 1) / 2.0)
    for i, b in enumerate(betas):
        g, _, c, _ = lse_grad(a, N, b)
        last_c = c
        w = (1.0 + i) / denom
        total += w * g
    total /= float(len(betas))
    if last_c is None:
        last_c = compute_conv_full(a, N)
    return total, last_c, float(last_c.max())


def lbfgs_dir(s_hist, y_hist, g):
    """L-BFGS two-loop recursion for a descent direction."""
    m = len(s_hist)
    n = len(g)
    gn = float(np.linalg.norm(g))
    if m == 0 or gn < 1e-18:
        if gn < 1e-18:
            return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        return -g / gn

    q = g.copy()
    alphas = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    for i in range(m - 1, -1, -1):
        s_i = s_hist[i]
        y_i = y_hist[i]
        yy = float(np.dot(y_i, y_i))
        if yy <= 1e-30:
            alphas[i] = 0.0
        else:
            alphas[i] = float(np.dot(s_i, q)) / yy
            q = q - alphas[i] * y_i

    s_last = s_hist[-1]
    y_last = y_hist[-1]
    yy_last = float(np.dot(y_last, y_last))
    if yy_last > 1e-30:
        gamma_use = max(1e-8, float(np.dot(s_last, y_last)) / yy_last)
    else:
        gamma_use = 1.0

    r = gamma_use * q
    for i in range(m):
        s_i = s_hist[i]
        y_i = y_hist[i]
        yy = float(np.dot(y_i, y_i))
        if yy <= 1e-30:
            continue
        beta_i = float(np.dot(s_i, y_i)) / yy
        r = r + (alphas[i] - beta_i) * y_i

    direction = -r
    if float(np.dot(g, direction)) > 0.0:
        direction = -g
    return direction


def spectral_partial_restart(a, N, rng, frac=0.5):
    """
    Randomize a fraction of FFT phases (preserving magnitudes), then
    project to the non-negative orthant and rescale to the current sum.
    """
    n = len(a)
    S = float(a.sum())
    A = fft(a, int(N))
    mag = np.abs(A)
    phase = np.angle(A)
    m = max(1, int(int(N) * float(frac)))
    idx = rng.choice(int(N), size=m, replace=False)
    phase[idx] = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi, size=m)
    A_new = mag * np.exp(1j * phase)
    a_new = np.real(ifft(A_new))[:n]
    a_new = np.where(np.isfinite(a_new), a_new, 0.0)
    a_new = np.maximum(a_new, 0.0)
    s = float(a_new.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return a.copy()
    return a_new * (S / s)


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Optimizers
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def lbfgs_lse_block(a_init, n, S, N, max_time, rng,
                    beta_init=5.0, beta_max=200.0,
                    m_lbfgs=10, use_multi_beta=False):
    """L-BFGS on LSE-smoothed objective with adaptive beta."""
    a = safe_normalize(a_init, n, S)
    beta = float(beta_init)

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_cmax = np.inf

    s_hist = []
    y_hist = []

    if use_multi_beta:
        betas = [beta, beta * 3.0, beta * 9.0]
        g, c, c_max = multi_beta_grad(a, N, betas)
    else:
        g, _, c, c_max = lse_grad(a, N, beta)

    if c_max < best_cmax:
        best_cmax = c_max
        best_a = a.copy()

    step_scale = 2.0 * S / (n * max(beta, 1.0))
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < max_time:
        it += 1

        if it % 150 == 0:
            beta = min(float(beta_max), beta * 1.10)
            step_scale = 2.0 * S / (n * max(beta, 1.0))
            if use_multi_beta:
                g, c, c_max = multi_beta_grad(a, N, [beta, beta * 3.0, beta * 9.0])
            else:
                g, _, c, c_max = lse_grad(a, N, beta)
            if c_max < best_cmax:
                best_cmax = c_max
                best_a = a.copy()

        if it % 40 == 0 and len(s_hist) >= m_lbfgs:
            s_hist = s_hist[-m_lbfgs:]
            y_hist = y_hist[-m_lbfgs:]

        direction = lbfgs_dir(s_hist, y_hist, g)
        dir_norm = float(np.linalg.norm(direction))
        if dir_norm < 1e-18:
            break

        alpha_ls = step_scale
        a_new = a
        cmax_new = c_max
        for _ls in range(12):
            cand = project_simplex(a + alpha_ls * direction, S)
            c_ls = compute_conv_full(cand, N)
            cm_ls = float(c_ls.max())
            if cm_ls <= c_max + 1e-14:
                a_new = cand
                cmax_new = cm_ls
                break
            alpha_ls *= 0.5

        if cmax_new < best_cmax:
            best_cmax = cmax_new
            best_a = a_new.copy()

        if use_multi_beta:
            g_new, _, cmax_nw = multi_beta_grad(a_new, N, [beta, beta * 3.0, beta * 9.0])
        else:
            g_new, _, _, cmax_nw = lse_grad(a_new, N, beta)

        s_k = a_new - a
        y_k = g_new - g
        sy = float(np.dot(s_k, y_k))
        ss = float(np.dot(s_k, s_k))
        if sy > 1e-14 * max(ss, 1e-30) and ss > 1e-30:
            s_hist.append(s_k)
            y_hist.append(y_k)
            if len(s_hist) > m_lbfgs:
                s_hist = s_hist[-m_lbfgs:]
                y_hist = y_hist[-m_lbfgs:]

        a = a_new
        g = g_new
        c_max = cmax_nw

        if it % 600 == 0 and rng.random() < 0.25:
            a = spectral_partial_restart(a, N, rng, frac=0.3)
            s_hist = []
            y_hist = []
            if use_multi_beta:
                g, _, c_max = multi_beta_grad(a, N, [beta, beta * 3.0, beta * 9.0])
            else:
                g, _, _, c_max = lse_grad(a, N, beta)

    return best_a, best_cmax


def nesterov_toppk_block(a_init, n, S, N, max_time, rng,
                         top_k_init=20, top_k_max=512):
    """Nesterov top-k subgradient polishing block."""
    a = safe_normalize(a_init, n, S)
    y = a.copy()
    t = 1.0
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_cmax = np.inf
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    it = 0
    fsize = 1 << (3 * n - 2).bit_length()

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < max_time:
        it += 1
        top_k = min(int(top_k_max), int(top_k_init) + it // 50)

        y = safe_normalize(np.maximum(y, 0.0), n, S)
        c = compute_conv_full(y, N)
        cur = float(c.max())
        if cur < best_cmax:
            best_cmax = cur
            best_a = y.copy()

        top_idx = np.argsort(c)[::-1][:top_k]
        top_vals = c[top_idx]
        total = float(top_vals.sum())
        if total <= 1e-30:
            break
        weights = top_vals / total

        w_full = np.zeros(fsize, dtype=np.float64)
        for i, k in enumerate(top_idx):
            w_full[int(k)] = weights[i]

        W = fft(w_full, fsize)
        Af = fft(y, fsize)
        grad = 2.0 * np.real(ifft(W * np.conj(Af)))[:n]
        grad = np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)
        gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
        if gnorm <= 1e-18:
            break

        step = 2.0 * S / (n * (1.0 + 0.003 * it))
        x_new = project_simplex(y - step * grad / gnorm, S)

        if it % 10 == 0:
            c_x = compute_conv_full(x_new, N)
            if float(c_x.max()) < best_cmax:
                best_cmax = float(c_x.max())
                best_a = x_new.copy()

        t_new = 0.5 * (1.0 + np.sqrt(1.0 + 4.0 * t * t))
        y = x_new + ((t - 1.0) / t_new) * (x_new - a)
        y = np.maximum(y, 0.0)
        y = safe_normalize(y, n, S)
        a = x_new
        t = t_new

        if it % 1500 == 0 and rng.random() < 0.15:
            y = spectral_partial_restart(y, N, rng, frac=0.2)
            y = safe_normalize(y, n, S)
            t = 1.0

    return best_a, best_cmax


def mirror_lse_block(a_init, n, S, N, max_time, rng,
                     beta_init=5.0, beta_max=200.0):
    """
    Multiplicative / mirror-descent style LSE block.
    The update is a_i <- a_i * exp(-eta * G_i) followed by renormalization.
    """
    a = safe_normalize(a_init, n, S)
    beta = float(beta_init)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_cmax = np.inf
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    it = 0
    fail = 0

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < max_time:
        it += 1

        if it % 120 == 0:
            beta = min(float(beta_max), beta * 1.12)

        g, _, c, cmax = lse_grad(a, N, beta)
        cmax = float(c.max())
        if cmax < best_cmax:
            best_cmax = cmax
            best_a = a.copy()

        gshift = g - float(g.min())
        gm = float(gshift.max())
        if gm <= 1e-18:
            break

        base_eta = 0.30 / gm
        accepted = False
        for mult in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.1, 0.2, 0.05, 2.0, 4.0):
            eta = base_eta * float(mult)
            if eta <= 0.0:
                continue
            logfac = -eta * gshift
            logfac = np.maximum(logfac, -700.0)
            b = a * np.exp(logfac)
            b = np.where(np.isfinite(b), b, 0.0)
            b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
            s = float(b.sum())
            if s <= 1e-15:
                continue
            b = b * (S / s)
            c_b = compute_conv_full(b, N)
            cb = float(c_b.max())
            if cb <= cmax + 1e-13:
                a = b
                cmax = cb
                accepted = True
                if cb < best_cmax:
                    best_cmax = cb
                    best_a = a.copy()
                break

        if not accepted:
            fail += 1
            if fail > 25:
                noise = rng.normal(0.0, 0.03, size=n)
                a = a * np.exp(noise)
                a = safe_normalize(a, n, S)
                fail = 0
        else:
            fail = 0

        if it % 700 == 0 and rng.random() < 0.20:
            a = spectral_partial_restart(a, N, rng, frac=0.25)
            a = safe_normalize(a, n, S)
            fail = 0

    return best_a, best_cmax


def peak_steepest_block(a_init, n, S, N, max_time, rng,
                        top_k_start=2, top_k_end=64):
    """
    Projected steepest descent on a uniform combination of the current top
    active convolution coefficients.
    """
    a = safe_normalize(a_init, n, S)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_cmax = np.inf
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    it = 0
    fail = 0

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < max_time:
        it += 1
        c = compute_conv_full(a, N)
        cmax = float(c.max())
        if cmax < best_cmax:
            best_cmax = cmax
            best_a = a.copy()

        K = min(int(top_k_end), int(top_k_start) + it // 20)
        top = np.argsort(c)[::-1][:K]

        G = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for k in top:
            k = int(k)
            i0 = max(0, k - (n - 1))
            i1 = min(k, n - 1)
            if i1 < i0:
                continue
            ii = np.arange(i0, i1 + 1, dtype=np.int64)
            jj = k - ii
            G[ii] += 2.0 * a[jj]

        G -= float(G.mean())
        gn = float(np.linalg.norm(G))
        if gn <= 1e-18:
            fail += 1
            continue

        d = -G / gn
        accepted = False
        for scale in (0.01, 0.03, 0.10, 0.003, 0.001, 0.30):
            step = float(scale) * S
            cand = project_simplex(a + step * d, S)
            c_new = compute_conv_full(cand, N)
            cm = float(c_new.max())
            if cm <= cmax + 1e-13:
                a = cand
                cmax = cm
                accepted = True
                if cm < best_cmax:
                    best_cmax = cm
                    best_a = a.copy()
                break

        if not accepted:
            fail += 1
            if fail > 35:
                noise = rng.normal(0.0, 0.025, size=n)
                a = a * np.exp(noise)
                a = safe_normalize(a, n, S)
                fail = 0
        else:
            fail = 0

    return best_a, best_cmax


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Low-dimensional analytic seed search
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def beta_family_sequence(n, p, q):
    """
    Generate a finite sequence from a Beta-like kernel:
        a_i ~ (i + 0.5)^(p-1) * (n-i-0.5)^(q-1)
    with p, q > 0.
    """
    try:
        i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
        left = i + 0.5
        right = n - i - 0.5
        if np.any(left <= 0.0) or np.any(right <= 0.0):
            return None
        loga = (float(p) - 1.0) * np.log(left) + (float(q) - 1.0) * np.log(right)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(loga)):
            return None
        loga -= float(loga.max())
        a = np.exp(loga)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
            return None
        return a
    except Exception:
        return None


def sweep_beta_family(n, S, N, max_time):
    """Cheap sweep over Beta/profile parameters to obtain strong seeds."""
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    best_a = None
    best_c = np.inf
    best_p = 0.5
    best_q = 1.0

    def consider_seq(a_raw):
        nonlocal best_a, best_c, best_p, best_q
        if a_raw is None:
            return
        a = safe_normalize(a_raw, n, S)
        c = compute_conv_full(a, N)
        cm = float(c.max())
        if np.isfinite(cm) and cm < best_c:
            best_c = cm
            best_a = a.copy()

    if max_time < 2.0:
        consider_seq(beta_family_sequence(n, 0.5, 1.0))
        consider_seq(beta_family_sequence(n, 0.5, 0.7))
        consider_seq(beta_family_sequence(n, 0.6, 1.0))
        return best_a, best_c, best_p, best_q

    coarse_p = [0.30, 0.40, 0.45, 0.50, 0.55, 0.60, 0.70, 0.80, 0.90, 1.00, 1.20, 1.50]
    coarse_q = [0.30, 0.40, 0.45, 0.50, 0.55, 0.60, 0.70, 0.80, 0.90, 1.00, 1.20, 1.50]

    for p in coarse_p:
        for q in coarse_q:
            if time.monotonic() - t0 > max_time:
                break
            a = beta_family_sequence(n, p, q)
            if a is None:
                continue
            a = safe_normalize(a, n, S)
            c = compute_conv_full(a, N)
            cm = float(c.max())
            if np.isfinite(cm) and cm < best_c:
                best_c = cm
                best_a = a.copy()
                best_p = p
                best_q = q

    if best_a is None:
        best_a = safe_normalize(beta_family_sequence(n, 0.5, 1.0), n, S)
        best_c = float(compute_conv_full(best_a, N).max())
        best_p = 0.5
        best_q = 1.0
        return best_a, best_c, best_p, best_q

    # Local refinement around the coarse best point.
    if time.monotonic() - t0 < max_time:
        dvals = [0.0, 0.05, -0.05, 0.10, -0.10, 0.15, -0.15]
        for dp in dvals:
            for dq in dvals:
                if time.monotonic() - t0 > max_time:
                    break
                p = best_p + dp
                q = best_q + dq
                if p <= 0.05 or q <= 0.05 or p > 2.2 or q > 2.2:
                    continue
                a = beta_family_sequence(n, p, q)
                if a is None:
                    continue
                a = safe_normalize(a, n, S)
                c = compute_conv_full(a, N)
                cm = float(c.max())
                if np.isfinite(cm) and cm < best_c:
                    best_c = cm
                    best_a = a.copy()
                    best_p = p
                    best_q = q

    return best_a, best_c, best_p, best_q


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Main search driver
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
    """
    Required entry point.  Searches for a valid non-negative sequence and
    returns it as a Python list.
    """
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    budget_s = float(budget_s)
    deadline = t_start + max(60.0, budget_s - 30.0)

    # Work with a reasonably large but fast length.
    n = 8192
    S = float(np.sqrt(2.0 * float(n)))
    N = 1 << (2 * n - 1).bit_length()

    print("n=%d S=%.4f N=%d budget=%.0fs" % (n, S, N, budget_s), flush=True)

    best_a = None
    best_cmax = np.inf

    # Seed from the global parent construction, if available and compatible.
    parent_a = None
    try:
        par = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
        if par.size == n and np.all(np.isfinite(par)):
            parent_a = safe_normalize(par, n, S)
            c_par = compute_conv_full(parent_a, N)
            c_par = float(c_par.max())
            if np.isfinite(c_par) and c_par < best_cmax:
                best_cmax = c_par
                best_a = parent_a.copy()
                print("parent seed score=%.6f" % best_cmax, flush=True)
    except Exception:
        parent_a = None

    # Cheap analytic seed sweep.
    sweep_budget = min(80.0, max(10.0, (deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.04))
    try:
        beta_a, beta_c, beta_p, beta_q = sweep_beta_family(n, S, N, sweep_budget)
        if beta_a is not None and np.isfinite(beta_c) and beta_c < best_cmax:
            best_cmax = beta_c
            best_a = beta_a.copy()
            print("beta seed (p=%.3f,q=%.3f) score=%.6f" % (beta_p, beta_q, best_cmax), flush=True)
    except Exception:
        beta_a = None
        beta_c = np.inf

    if best_a is None:
        best_a = safe_normalize(beta_family_sequence(n, 0.5, 1.0), n, S)
        best_cmax = float(compute_conv_full(best_a, N).max())

    # Optional blending of parent and analytic seeds.
    if parent_a is not None and beta_a is not None:
        for w in (0.0, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0):
            blend = w * parent_a + (1.0 - w) * beta_a
            blend = safe_normalize(blend, n, S)
            cb = float(compute_conv_full(blend, N).max())
            if np.isfinite(cb) and cb < best_cmax:
                best_cmax = cb
                best_a = blend.copy()
                print("blend w=%.2f score=%.6f" % (w, best_cmax), flush=True)

    # Define a small helper list of phases.  No nested functions.
    # Each phase is implemented by calling the corresponding top-level function.

    rem = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
    if rem > 220.0:
        try:
            a, c = lbfgs_lse_block(best_a, n, S, N, 220.0, rng,
                                   beta_init=5.0, beta_max=250.0,
                                   m_lbfgs=12, use_multi_beta=True)
            if c < best_cmax:
                best_cmax = c
                best_a = a
                print("LBFGS#1 score=%.6f" % best_cmax, flush=True)
        except Exception as e:
            print("lbfgs1 failed: %s" % e, flush=True)

    rem = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
    if rem > 140.0:
        try:
            a, c = peak_steepest_block(best_a, n, S, N, 140.0, rng,
                                       top_k_start=2, top_k_end=48)
            if c < best_cmax:
                best_cmax = c
                best_a = a
                print("peak#1 score=%.6f" % best_cmax, flush=True)
        except Exception as e:
            print("peak1 failed: %s" % e, flush=True)

    rem = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
    if rem > 140.0:
        try:
            a, c = mirror_lse_block(best_a, n, S, N, 140.0, rng,
                                    beta_init=5.0, beta_max=350.0)
            if c < best_cmax:
                best_cmax = c
                best_a = a
                print("mirror#1 score=%.6f" % best_cmax, flush=True)
        except Exception as e:
            print("mirror1 failed: %s" % e, flush=True)

    rem = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
    if rem > 140.0:
        try:
            a, c = nesterov_toppk_block(best_a, n, S, N, 140.0, rng,
                                        top_k_init=10, top_k_max=256)
            if c < best_cmax:
                best_cmax = c
                best_a = a
                print("nesterov#1 score=%.6f" % best_cmax, flush=True)
        except Exception as e:
            print("nesterov1 failed: %s" % e, flush=True)

    rem = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
    if rem > 300.0:
        try:
            a, c = lbfgs_lse_block(best_a, n, S, N, 300.0, rng,
                                   beta_init=20.0, beta_max=900.0,
                                   m_lbfgs=16, use_multi_beta=False)
            if c < best_cmax:
                best_cmax = c
                best_a = a
                print("LBFGS#2 high-beta score=%.6f" % best_cmax, flush=True)
        except Exception as e:
            print("lbfgs2 failed: %s" % e, flush=True)

    rem = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
    if rem > 120.0:
        try:
            a, c = peak_steepest_block(best_a, n, S, N, 120.0, rng,
                                       top_k_start=1, top_k_end=24)
            if c < best_cmax:
                best_cmax = c
                best_a = a
                print("peak#2 score=%.6f" % best_cmax, flush=True)
        except Exception as e:
            print("peak2 failed: %s" % e, flush=True)

    rem = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
    if rem > 140.0:
        try:
            a, c = mirror_lse_block(best_a, n, S, N, 140.0, rng,
                                    beta_init=25.0, beta_max=1000.0)
            if c < best_cmax:
                best_cmax = c
                best_a = a
                print("mirror#2 score=%.6f" % best_cmax, flush=True)
        except Exception as e:
            print("mirror2 failed: %s" % e, flush=True)

    rem = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
    if rem > 140.0:
        try:
            a, c = nesterov_toppk_block(best_a, n, S, N, 140.0, rng,
                                        top_k_init=20, top_k_max=512)
            if c < best_cmax:
                best_cmax = c
                best_a = a
                print("nesterov#2 score=%.6f" % best_cmax, flush=True)
        except Exception as e:
            print("nesterov2 failed: %s" % e, flush=True)

    # Small multi-start polish: a few perturbations of the current best.
    rem = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
    if rem > 360.0:
        for sigma, alloc in ((0.02, 70.0), (0.05, 90.0), (0.10, 120.0)):
            rem = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
            if rem < alloc + 20.0:
                continue
            noise = rng.normal(0.0, sigma, size=n)
            perturbed = best_a * np.exp(noise)
            perturbed = safe_normalize(perturbed, n, S)
            try:
                a, c = lbfgs_lse_block(perturbed, n, S, N, alloc, rng,
                                       beta_init=15.0, beta_max=700.0,
                                       m_lbfgs=14, use_multi_beta=False)
                if c < best_cmax:
                    best_cmax = c
                    best_a = a
                    print("multistart sigma=%.2f score=%.6f" % (sigma, best_cmax), flush=True)
            except Exception as e:
                print("multistart failed: %s" % e, flush=True)

    # Final long polish if time remains.
    rem = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 15.0)
    if rem > 60.0:
        try:
            a, c = lbfgs_lse_block(best_a, n, S, N, rem, rng,
                                   beta_init=30.0, beta_max=1200.0,
                                   m_lbfgs=16, use_multi_beta=False)
            if c < best_cmax:
                best_cmax = c
                best_a = a
                print("final LBFGS score=%.6f" % best_cmax, flush=True)
        except Exception as e:
            print("final lbfgs failed: %s" % e, flush=True)

    # One very short mirror polish at the end.
    rem = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 5.0)
    if rem > 30.0:
        try:
            a, c = mirror_lse_block(best_a, n, S, N, min(rem, 60.0), rng,
                                    beta_init=50.0, beta_max=1500.0)
            if c < best_cmax:
                best_cmax = c
                best_a = a
                print("final mirror score=%.6f" % best_cmax, flush=True)
        except Exception as e:
            print("final mirror failed: %s" % e, flush=True)

    # Fallback if something went wrong.
    if best_a is None or not np.all(np.isfinite(best_a)):
        best_a = np.full(n, S / n, dtype=np.float64)
        best_cmax = float(compute_conv_full(best_a, N).max())

    best_a = np.clip(best_a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if float(best_a.sum()) < 0.01:
        best_a = np.full(256, 1.0, dtype=np.float64)
        best_cmax = float(compute_conv_full(best_a, 256).max())

    final_list = [float(x) for x in best_a]

    # Verify with the official evaluator when available.
    try:
        val = evaluate_sequence(final_list)
        if np.isfinite(val):
            print("verified evaluate_sequence=%.6f internal=%.6f" % (val, best_cmax), flush=True)
            if val > 2.0:
                # If official verification is much worse (should not happen),
                # fall back to a very safe uniform sequence.
                fallback = np.full(256, 1.0, dtype=np.float64)
                final_list = [float(x) for x in fallback]
        else:
            print("official evaluator returned non-finite; using fallback", flush=True)
            fallback = np.full(256, 1.0, dtype=np.float64)
            final_list = [float(x) for x in fallback]
    except Exception:
        # evaluate_sequence may not be defined in some sandboxes; internal
        # score is still used for selection.
        print("internal score only: %.6f" % best_cmax, flush=True)

    return final_list
```
